Wires the SpecForge prepare_hidden_states -&gt; train_eagle3 / train_dflash -&gt; convert-to-vLLM flow on Slurm, then gates on a measured acceptance-length A/B (dflash_vllm_eval.py + vLLM spec_decode_* counters) vs the standing config. - **inflate**: Inflates plain facts or boring announcements into full LinkedIn thought-leadership glory — epiphany arc, fake paradox, fortune-cookie lesson, comment-farming question, the works. Use when the user gives a plain fact, event, or draft and wants it enshittified, corporatized, LinkedIn-ified, or turned into engagement-bait satire. - **Inflation Analysis**: Measure and explain inflation with frequency and base-period clarity. ([rikitrader/glaw](https://github.com/rikitrader/glaw/tree/HEAD/departments/hanke-economics/skills/inflation-analysis) · ★5)
- **inflation-get-inflation**: Returns the latest annual CPI inflation rate for a country plus the previous 10 years of historical data. ([bobadilla-tech/requiems-api-skills](https://github.com/bobadilla-tech/requiems-api-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/inflation-get-inflation) · ★2)
- **inflation-post-batch**: Returns inflation data for up to 50 countries in a single request. Results are in the same order as the input.
